### [66] [The Lazy Pod That Lies: Deferred Cost and Failure Semantics of Lazy Container Image Pulling for Model Serving on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19412)
*Georgii Kliukovkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在Kubernetes上使用KServe对两种生产级懒加载容器镜像拉取系统（eStargz/stargz-snapshotter和AWS SOCI）与急切拉取基线进行了系统评估，覆盖2到140GB的真实fp16权重模型。发现懒加载确实实现了冷启动时间与模型大小无关（16.9-17.6秒 vs 急切拉取的24.5-573.0秒），但成本只是被延迟而非消除，并揭示了一种急切拉取在结构上不可能出现的失败模式：快照器节点级缓存在持续合法读取下耗尽，导致已运行pod的模型文件读取失败，且存在可见性与实际故障不一致的隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型服务pod的启动成本主要由容器镜像拉取主导，懒加载容器镜像技术承诺通过立即挂载和按需获取内容来消除这一成本。然而该承诺在生产环境中的实际表现、权衡和潜在失效模式尚未被系统评估，尤其是针对大型AI模型的场景。

Method: 在Kubernetes上使用KServe搭建模型服务环境，选取eStargz/stargz-snapshotter和AWS SOCI两种生产级懒加载系统，与急切拉取设置对比，使用从2GB到140GB的工件（含真实fp16权重）进行冷启动时间、全量读取性能、缓存生命周期等维度的系统化基准测试。

Result: 懒加载实现了冷pod首次预测时间与大小无关（16.9-17.6秒vs急切拉取的24.5-573.0秒）；但14GB模型的懒加载完整读取耗时105.3秒，慢于急切拉取的72.4秒，成本只是被延迟。两种系统在生命周期两端付代价：SOCI在Ready前预取几乎全部镜像，eStargz将几乎所有内容推迟到首次读取。更严重的是发现了节点级缓存耗尽的失败模式：在持续合法读取下，快照器缓存耗尽导致运行中pod读取失败，最早期阶段pod在196秒内通过所有Kubernetes及应用级检查但快照器已记录真实故障；高压力下67-94%模型文件失败。释放缓存空间可自愈，但重启守护进程会使运行中pod的文件句柄永久失效而pod仍显示Running。

Conclusion: 懒加载镜像拉取能实现其核心承诺（大小无关的快速冷启动），但运维人员需要认识到成本只是被延迟而非消除，且需警惕节点级缓存耗尽这一独特失败模式。论文提出了针对服务平台的放置、监控和缓存大小配置指导。

Abstract: Lazy container-image pulling promises to eliminate the dominant cost of starting a model-serving pod by mounting the image immediately and fetching content on demand. We evaluate this promise for model delivery on Kubernetes, using KServe with two production lazy-pulling systems -- eStargz/stargz-snapshotter and AWS SOCI -- against eager baselines, on artifacts from 2 to 140 GB including real fp16 weights. Lazy pulling delivers its headline: cold time-to-first-prediction becomes size-independent (16.9--17.6s, versus 24.5--573.0s eager). But the cost is deferred, not eliminated: a full read of a 14 GB model through the lazy mount takes 105.3s, slower than the 72.4s eager pull it replaced, and the two systems pay at opposite lifecycle ends (SOCI prefetches nearly the full image before Ready; eStargz defers nearly everything to first read). More consequentially, we characterize a failure mode eager pulling structurally cannot exhibit: under sustained legitimate reads with default configuration, the snapshotter's node-level cache exhausts its finite volume and already-running pods begin failing reads of model files. At the earliest stage of exhaustion, an instrumented serving pod passed every Kubernetes-visible and application-level check for 196s while its snapshotter was already logging real failures; under heavier pressure, 67--94% of model files fail, scaling monotonically with residual cache occupancy. A live pod self-heals if cache space is freed under it, but a snapshotter-daemon restart under a live pod leaves permanently stale file handles in a pod still reported Running. We derive placement, monitoring, and cache-sizing guidance for serving platforms and operators.

</details>


### [67] [When Do LLM Agents Help? Deadline-Aware Mixed-Criticality Task Scheduling at the Autonomous-Vehicle Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19557)
*Reza Zakerian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究异构MEC服务器上截止时间感知的混合关键度调度问题，构建了窗口化合同网拍卖启发式算法（TC完成率0.902，优于15个基线），并系统评估多智能体LLM控制层的增量价值，发现LLM仅在任务负载非平稳（中期安全关键任务激增）时才显著优于静态启发式与bandit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶将延迟敏感的感知任务卸载到MEC服务器，错过安全关键任务是不能容忍的。LLM被越来越多地用作自适应、可解释的调度器，但缺乏证据表明它们在何时真正有助益。需要严谨评估LLM控制层相比强启发式算法的实际增量收益。

Method: 两步法：(1)构建强启发式——窗口化合同网拍卖，每个准入窗口按最早截止时间优先（TC-first）排序，按最早完成时间（EFT）放置任务；(2)加上LLM控制平面，采用受控分解分析将调度器的优势追溯到批量视界（batching horizon）与TC-first排序两个普通因素，并在平稳负载与非平稳负载（安全关键任务激增）两种场景下对比静态启发式、bandit与LLM策略。

Result: 在3种拓扑、60个实例和15个基线上，启发式算法TC完成率达0.902，超过所有基线（Holm校正p<0.001，最佳基线0.838），达到CP-SAT上界的0.87。在平稳负载下启发式已接近最优，拍卖机制、逐窗口LLM策略和在线自适应均无额外收益；但在中期安全关键任务激增时，LLM控制平面显著优于静态启发式和bandit。

Conclusion: LLM编排只有在非平稳性为固定策略无法利用的调度留有空间时才值得其计算成本。研究提供了控制平面延迟与决策解释，并发布了全部代码和带种子的实例。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles offload latency-sensitive perception tasks to nearby mobile edge computing (MEC) servers, where a missed safety-critical task is unsafe rather than merely degraded. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as adaptive, explainable schedulers, yet evidence of when they help is scarce. We study deadline-aware, mixed-criticality scheduling on heterogeneous MEC servers, where time-critical (TC) tasks must be protected at a controlled cost to best-effort traffic, and ask whether a multi-agent LLM control layer improves on a strong heuristic. We answer in two steps. First we build the heuristic: a windowed contract-net auction that orders each admission window time-critical-first by earliest deadline and places tasks by earliest-finish-time. Across 60 instances on three topologies and 15 baselines under an identical online constraint, it attains a TC completion rate of 0.902, above every baseline (Holm-corrected p < 0.001; best baseline 0.838) and at 0.87 of a CP-SAT upper bound. Second, we add the LLM control plane. A controlled decomposition traces the scheduler's advantage to two ordinary factors, the batching horizon and time-critical-first ordering; the auction, the per-window LLM policy, and online adaptation add nothing while the load is stationary, where the heuristic is already near-optimal. Under a mid-run surge of safety-critical tasks the picture changes, and the LLM control plane gains significantly over both the static heuristic and the bandit. LLM orchestration therefore earns its cost only when non-stationarity opens headroom a fixed policy cannot use. We report control-plane latency and rationale, and release all code and seeded instances.

</details>


### [68] [Optimality and Trade-offs in Fast BFT SMR (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19629)
*Neil Giridharan,Minh Tung Nguyen,Ittai Abraham,Allen Clement,Natacha Crooks,Pierre Sutra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对快速拜占庭容错（Fast BFT）状态机复制协议，给出了副本因子（replication factor）的精确上下界，并提出一个次优协议来展示副本因子与恢复效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速状态机复制（Fast SMR）协议在崩溃容错场景下已得到广泛研究，但在拜占庭容错场景下理解尚不充分，已有的快速BFT SMR协议数量很少。本文旨在填补这一空白，明确快速BFT SMR所需的最小副本因子。

Method: 通过理论分析推导快速BFT SMR协议副本因子的紧上下界，同时设计一个次优但恢复效率更高的协议，用来展示副本因子与恢复效率之间的权衡关系。

Result: 得到了快速BFT SMR所需的副本因子精确（紧）上下界，并提出一个次优协议，该协议在副本因子上有所牺牲但换取了更好的恢复效率。

Conclusion: 快速BFT SMR的副本因子存在紧界，且副本因子与恢复效率之间存在内在权衡，为后续快速BFT协议设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Fast state-machine replication (SMR) protocols in the crash-fault setting have attracted significant interest in both academia and industry. This interest stems from their advantages over leader-based protocols, including low execution latency for non-conflicting commands, high throughput, improved availability, and increased fairness. While Fast SMR is well studied in the crash-fault setting, the Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) setting remains much less understood, with only a small number of existing Fast BFT SMR protocols. In this paper, we present tight upper and lower bounds on the replication factor required for Fast BFT SMR. We also present a suboptimal protocol that illustrates a trade-off between replication factor and recovery efficiency.

</details>


### [69] [CacheRoute: Planned Prefix-Affinity Routing for Large-Scale LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19677)
*Huang Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CacheRoute通过周期性路由规划解决前缀缓存亲和性与负载均衡之间的权衡，在Llama-3.3-70B fp8、60块H100 GPU上实现176±11 QPS的吞吐量，较最强基线提升2.3倍，KV缓存命中率从64.1%提升至93.2%，并发现利用缓存亲和性的部署需通过影子回放进行门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存（prefix caching）仅在重复请求回到仍持有前缀KV的服务器时才避免prefill。缓存盲均衡（cache-blind balancing）会分散缓存复用，而固定亲和性虽然能保留复用但可能导致服务器过载。现有方法无法同时兼顾缓存命中率和负载均衡，亟需一种新的路由机制来解决这一权衡问题。

Method: CacheRoute采用周期性路由规划方案：将高频率key纳入稳定的热集（warm set），并根据预期负载分配其目标服务器；热key可使用多个目标节点，但实验中的主半合成聚合数据中每个key仅使用单一目标。通过路由规划在保证缓存亲和性的同时实现负载均衡。

Result: 在Llama-3.3-70B fp8、60块H100 GPU上，CacheRoute在3.5秒p99 SLO下维持176±11 QPS，为五个基线中最强者的2.3倍。KV缓存命中率从缓存盲均衡下的64.1±1.3%提升至93.2±0.5%。通过第二组半合成聚合数据及受控8B和突发实验验证了亲和性与放置策略的各自效果。两个32B工作负载构成反例：当亲和性恢复的KV工作量过少时，残余负载偏差会减少或消除改进。

Conclusion: CacheRoute有效解决了前缀缓存路由中的亲和性与负载均衡之间的权衡，但并非所有场景都受益于缓存亲和性。建议在部署任何缓存亲和性方案前，使用影子回放（shadow replay）进行门控验证，而非仅依据工作负载统计特征直接启用。

Abstract: Prefix caching avoids prefill only when a repeated request returns to a server that still holds the prefix KV. Cache-blind balancing disperses that reuse; fixed affinity preserves it but can overload a server. CacheRoute resolves this tradeoff with a periodic routing plan. It admits high-rate keys to a stable warm set and places their assignments by expected load. Hot keys may use more than one destination, although every key in our primary semi-synthetic aggregate uses exactly one. On Llama-3.3-70B in fp8 across 60 H100 GPUs, CacheRoute sustains 176+/-11 QPS at a 3.5-s p99 SLO, 2.3x the strongest of five baselines. Served KV-cache hit rate rises from 64.1+/-1.3% under cache-blind balancing to 93.2+/-0.5%. A second semi-synthetic aggregate and controlled 8B and burst experiments separate the effects of affinity and placement. Two 32B workloads provide the counterexamples: when affinity recovers too little KV work, its residual load skew reduces or erases the improvement. We therefore recommend gating any deployment with a shadow replay rather than enabling affinity from workload statistics alone.

</details>


### [70] [ParaWeb: Parallel Programming Patterns for Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19935)
*Suejb Memeti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ParaWeb，一个TypeScript库，为Node.js、浏览器和WebGPU环境实现了十种并行编程模式，每种模式提供消息传递（MP）、共享缓冲区（Shared）和GPU三种实现变体，共三十个实现。CPU变体在16线程下最高获得11.6倍加速，GPU变体在高算术强度的计算密集型模式中最高获得260倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web应用需要大量的计算密集型处理，但JavaScript传统上受限于单线程执行模型。虽然Node.js Worker Threads和浏览器Web Workers提供了底层并行机制，但开发者缺乏能够将常见并行结构抽象为可复用模式的高级抽象。

Method: ParaWeb实现十种并行编程模式（包括Farm、Scatter、Reduce、Map等），每种模式提供三种变体：基于postMessage结构化克隆的消息传递（MP）变体、基于SharedArrayBuffer和类型化数组视图的共享缓冲区（Shared）变体、以及使用WebGPU计算着色器实现硬件加速的GPU变体。跨越Node.js服务端、浏览器客户端和WebGPU三种执行环境。

Result: 实验评估覆盖全部三十个实现在三种数据规模下的性能。CPU变体在16线程下对计算密集型模式取得最高11.6倍加速；GPU变体对高算术强度的计算密集型模式（如Farm、Scatter、Reduce和Map）取得最高260倍加速。五类图像卷积滤波器的案例研究中，GPU加速在不可分离内核上达到最高414倍加速，且在1024x1024、2048x2048和4K图像上表现出一致的扩展性。

Conclusion: ParaWeb提供了覆盖多环境、多模式、多变体的Web并行编程框架，验证了在Node.js、浏览器和WebGPU三种环境中实现高效并行计算的可行性，尤其证明了GPU变体在高算术强度计算任务中的显著优势，为Web开发者提供了实用的高抽象并行编程工具。

Abstract: Modern web applications increasingly require computationally intensive processing, yet JavaScript, the dominant language of the web, has traditionally been limited to a single-threaded execution model. Node.js Worker Threads and browser Web Workers provide low-level mechanisms for parallel execution, but developers lack high-level abstractions that capture recurring parallel structures as reusable patterns. In this paper, we present ParaWeb, a TypeScript library that implements ten parallel programming patterns for server-side Node.js, client-side browser environments, and WebGPU compute shaders. ParaWeb provides three implementation variants for each pattern: a message-passing (MP) variant based on structured cloning via postMessage, a shared-buffer (Shared) variant that uses SharedArrayBuffer with typed array views, and a GPU variant that uses WebGPU compute shaders for hardware-accelerated execution. We describe the architecture, design decisions, and pattern-specific implementation strategies, and we evaluate the performance of all thirty implementations across three data sizes. Experimental evaluation results show that the CPU-based variants achieve speedups of up to 11.6x with 16 threads for compute-bound patterns, while the GPU variants reach speedups of up to 260x for compute-bound patterns with high arithmetic intensity such as Farm, Scatter, Reduce, and Map. A case study on five image-convolution filters further shows that GPU acceleration reaches up to 414x speedup over single-threaded CPU on non-separable kernels, with consistent scaling across 1024x1024$, 2048x2048$, and 4K images.

</details>


### [71] [Design and Empirical Evaluation of a Network-Centric, On-Premises Architecture for Earth Observation Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20283)
*João Pinelo,João Gonçalves,Denis Willett,Amit Ruhela,Derek Steinmoeller,Uriel Mendoza,Pelumi S. Alao,Ronald Soares Lopes,Rogerio Atem de Carvalho,Pedro Mattos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种面向本地部署（on-premises）地球观测数据访问的网络中心型架构，并在其首个实际部署（AIR数据中心，大西洋云的创始节点）中进行了评测。系统由基于100 GbE网络的MinIO对象存储集群、PostGIS元数据目录和OGC API-EDR访问层组成。研究发现网络带宽是批量EO数据访问存储吞吐量的主导约束，存在一个阈值（本硬件级别上每服务器高于10 Gbps）；低于该阈值时网络容量直接决定设施可交付的性能，超过后部署更先进的端点内存拓扑收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测项目产生的数据量超出了大多数机构网络的传输和存储能力。虽然公共云平台能服务资源充足的机构，但大西洋盆地的机构面临连接性、主权和资金限制，使得本地基础设施成为唯一可行路径。云原生数据格式虽支持高效部分读取，但其性能强烈依赖底层网络带宽，而这种依赖关系很少被独立测量，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者构建了一个可复制的网络中心型本地EO数据访问架构，包含三个核心组件：MinIO对象存储集群运行于100 GbE网络架构之上、PostGIS元数据目录、OGC API-EDR访问层。评测方法包括：在持续的并行负载下表征网络架构、评估对象存储在EO代表性工作负载下的吞吐量、以及在相同硬件上对比节流基线，将网络带宽隔离为唯一变量。此外还通过与合作伙伴机构的多站点复制基准测试来表征联邦原语。

Result: 结果表明，网络带宽是批量EO数据访问存储吞吐量的主导约束，但存在一个阈值：在本硬件级别上该阈值位于每服务器10 Gbps以上。低于该阈值时，网络容量单独决定了设施可交付的性能；高于该阈值时，进一步网络投资的回报取决于端点内存拓扑（而非容量），而内存配置可以延迟到后续再采购。

Conclusion: 对于受资源约束的机构而言，在本地部署EO数据基础设施时，网络带宽是先决条件资源，而端点内存的升级可以按需延期。该架构在AIR数据中心得到了首个实际部署验证，证明了其可复制性和联邦扩展能力，为大西洋盆地的机构提供了一条不依赖公共云的可行路径。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) programmes generate data at volumes that exceed the transfer and storage capacity of most institutional networks. Public cloud platforms address this for well-resourced organisations, but institutions across the Atlantic basin face constraints in connectivity, sovereignty and funding that make on-premises infrastructure the only viable path. Cloud-native data formats enable efficient partial reads, yet their performance depends on the bandwidth of the underlying network fabric, a dependency rarely measured in isolation. This paper presents a replicable, network-centric architecture for on-premises EO data access, evaluated at its first operational deployment: the AIR Data Centre, founding node of the Atlantic Cloud. The system comprises a MinIO object storage cluster on a 100 GbE fabric, a PostGIS metadata catalogue and an OGC API-EDR access layer. We characterise the fabric under sustained parallel load, evaluate object storage throughput for EO-representative workloads, and compare measured performance against throttled baselines on identical hardware, isolating network bandwidth as the sole variable. Multi-site replication benchmarks with partner institutions characterise the federation primitive the model depends on. Network bandwidth is the dominant constraint on storage throughput for bulk EO data access up to a threshold; beyond it, endpoint memory topology rather than capacity governs how much bandwidth a system can use. For this hardware class that threshold lies above 10 Gbps per server. Below it, network capacity alone sets what the facility can deliver; above it, the return on further network investment depends on endpoint memory provisioning, which can be deferred and bought later.

</details>

### [72] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将交互式AI智能体的上下文获取问题形式化为主动推断（active inference），在默认假设与花费token进行澄清、检索、工具调用或提示试验之间做出权衡。提出了最优提问（OQA）框架，使用精确后验和动态规划oracle进行实例化，并在25到300个候选的二元及多元分类任务上评测了前沿语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI智能体必须尽可能高效地获取正确的上下文信息。当用户省略约束、偏好、文件或任务变量时，智能体可以基于默认假设继续，也可以花费token进行澄清提问、检索调用、工具调用或提示试验。需要一种形式化的框架来量化并优化这一权衡。

Method: 将上下文获取建模为主动推断：内部推断步骤更新对潜在任务状态的信念，外部决策步骤选择下一个上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作，以在成本约束下最小化期望自由能。在确定性设置中，认知项简化为期望信息增益，并可选地按token成本归一化。将该框架实例化为最优提问（OQA），采用精确后验和动态规划oracle。

Result: 在25到300个候选的二元和多元分类任务上基准测试了前沿语言模型，并研究了token预算下的生成前澄清和自动提示优化。

Conclusion: 该形式化是模型无关的，将主动推断视为AI智能体上下文获取层的设计原则。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [73] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首篇聚焦于不确定环境下鲁棒种群元启发式算法求解泊位分配与岸桥调度问题（BACAP）的综述论文，从机制导向视角系统梳理现有方法，并提出基准套件与基线实验结果，最后指出开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BACAP是港口码头调度中的代表性难题，在到达偏差、处理时间波动和资源中断等不确定性下，基于名义假设优化的调度方案在执行中可能变得脆弱。尽管种群元启发式算法已被广泛用于解决BACAP，但现有研究在不确定性表示、鲁棒性准则、搜索机制和实证评估协议等方面较为分散，缺乏系统性梳理。

Method: 本文采用机制导向的综述框架，首先总结BACAP中的不确定性来源与信息表示，然后从四个维度组织现有方法：解表示与解码、鲁棒评估与选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动力学、可行性保持与恢复。同时构建了一个面向不确定BACAP的基准测试套件，并报告了代表性元启发式算法与不同鲁棒性策略组合的基线实验结果。

Result: 提出了首个聚焦于鲁棒种群元启发式算法求解不确定BACAP的综述，提供了系统化的方法分类框架和基准套件，并给出了组合基线实验结果。

Conclusion: 指出了包括基准扩展、鲁棒性感知搜索设计、时间自适应鲁棒性和非平稳不确定性等开放挑战，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [74] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将人工智能意识（AI consciousness）这一难以解决的问题，转换为人工智能效价（AI valence）这一可操作的问题，以应对潜在有感知能力AI的道德对待困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关于人工智能意识可能性存在深度不确定性的背景下，我们面临两难困境：假设AI无感知可能对值得道德地位的实体造成严重伤害，而假设AI有感知则可能浪费资源在无感知的机器上。由于AI意识问题本质上的不可解性，这一困境难以回避。

Method: 作者提出将不可解的AI意识问题转换为可解的AI效价问题——即评估AI是否具有那些如果它有意识就会构成效价体验的状态。作者论证了这一转换足以奠基一种负责任地对待潜在有意识AI的开发方法。

Result: 作者表明，通过聚焦于AI效价而非AI意识，可以为潜在有感知AI的开发提供负责任的道德基础，从而规避原有两难困境。

Conclusion: 将问题焦点从AI意识转移到AI效价，是走出两难困境的有效路径，能够为潜在有意识AI的开发提供负责任的伦理框架。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [75] [From Retrieved Context to Runtime Control: Adaptive Compression for Edge-based RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19535)
*Zlatan Feric,Amir Taherin,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于遥测信息的自适应压缩策略用于边缘RAG系统。通过在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor上的实测（Llama/Qwen生成器、Natural Questions/HotpotQA数据集、LLMLingua-2压缩器），发现存在自适应工作区域：过度压缩损害推理质量，轻度压缩浪费能量机会，而中等压缩可在几乎无质量损失的情况下将GPU能耗降低53.2%、SoC能耗降低48.2%。作者主张用运行时策略根据工作负载特征和边缘设备遥测状态动态管理压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过检索外部段落增强语言模型生成，但检索到的上下文会显著增加提示长度，带来KV缓存、预填充、延迟和能耗开销。上下文压缩虽可缓解此问题，但现有方法使用固定压缩预算或离线选择压缩率，忽视了工作负载变化和边缘设备实时状态。由于压缩器本身运行在同一边缘SoC上，其自身也消耗延迟和能量，固定压缩策略可能得不偿失。

Method: 作者在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor边缘平台上进行了系统的实验表征：使用Llama和Qwen作为生成器，Natural Questions和HotpotQA作为数据集，LLMLingua-2作为压缩器。通过测量不同压缩率下的生成延迟、GPU能耗和SoC总能耗，刻画压缩率-质量-能耗之间的权衡关系，并识别出自适应工作区域。

Result: 实验结果显示：对7B-8B规模的大模型，生成阶段占单查询延迟的约90%、GPU能耗的约91%。压缩率的影响呈现自适应操作区域：轻度过小的压缩率会错失能耗优化机会，而过度激进的压缩会损害推理质量。在中等压缩区间，GPU能耗可降低53.2%，SoC总能耗可降低48.2%，且质量损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 作者提出应从静态压缩策略转向由遥测信息驱动的自适应压缩策略，即运行时根据工作负载特征和边缘设备实时状态（如功耗、热状态、资源利用率等）动态调整压缩率，以在推理质量和资源消耗之间取得最优平衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language-model responses by grounding generation in external passages, which comes with overhead: retrieved context lengthens the prompt, increasing prefill work, KV-cache footprint, memory traffic, latency, and energy. Context compression offers a natural remedy by pruning retrieved text before generation. However, state-of-the-art context-compression methods are typically used with a fixed compression budget, or with the rate selected offline and then applied at inference time. This static view ignores both workload variation and the live state of the edge device. On an edge SoC, compression is not free: the compressor itself runs on the same SoC and consumes latency and energy that can offset any generation savings.
  This paper proposes a vision for telemetry-informed adaptive compression in edge RAG, grounded in experimental evidence. We characterize the compression tradeoff on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor using Llama and Qwen generators, Natural Questions and HotpotQA datasets, and LLMLingua-2 compression. Our measurements show that generation dominates the RAG budget for larger models, reaching roughly 90% of per-query latency and 91% of GPU energy for 7B-8B generators. Exploring the impact of the compression rate reveals an adaptive operating region: mild compression can miss energy opportunities, and overly aggressive compression can hurt inference quality. Intermediate compression can reduce GPU energy by up to 53.2%, and SoC energy by up to 48.2%, with negligible quality loss. We argue for runtime policies that dynamically manage compression, guided by workload features and edge telemetry.

</details>


### [76] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界主权"概念，分析受监管组织通过API使用前沿模型时，因只能控制业务流程而无法控制模型、基础设施和日志等层面，导致许多AI控制协议无法执行的问题。论文贡献了四层访问类型学、协议-层需求矩阵、主权折扣成本概念，并基于135万次合成案例仿真和匿名国家支付基础设施场景进行验证，呼吁控制协议明确其访问假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI控制研究大多假设部署者能够对模型及其周围管线进行全面检测，但在受监管组织通过API或托管端点使用前沿模型的场景中，部署者往往只能控制业务流程，而无法控制模型权重、服务基础设施、内部追踪、更新过程或完整交互日志。这种访问权限的不匹配导致许多控制协议在实际中无法执行，论文旨在填补这一理论与实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: 论文提出四层访问类型学（数据层、模型层、基础设施层、交互层）来描述AI栈中不同层面的访问条件；构建协议-层需求矩阵将控制协议与所需访问条件对应起来；提出"主权折扣成本"概念量化因缺少访问而付出的替代成本。在实验上，论文进行了一次合成的访问消融实验，包含135万个合成案例仿真，并通过匿名化的国家支付基础设施场景对发现进行解释性验证，明确声明该实验属于构造效度验证而非真实支付系统证据。

Result: 实验结果显示：完整日志能够改善诊断能力；预执行网关能够实现干预；追踪访问和模型版本控制能加强事后解释能力；范围限制可以在提升安全性的同时降低实用性。这些结果说明不同层面的访问权限直接影响控制协议的有效性。

Conclusion: 控制协议如果被作为通用安全解决方案提出，应当明确陈述其访问假设。论文提出的"有界主权"框架为评估和选择控制协议提供了基于实际访问条件的分析工具，帮助受监管组织判断哪些控制协议在自身的部署约束下是可行的。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [77] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于价值（valence）感知的社会网络框架，通过姿态计算机视觉流水线从商业奶牛场挤奶区7小时39分钟的视频中提取互动，构建亲和行为与对抗行为的层级网络表示，并揭示了将互动按价值分离后形成的网络层在边集、社群划分和个体位置上存在显著差异，表明聚合互动计数可能掩盖网络的行为构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会关系塑造了资源获取、冲突暴露和群体稳定性，但现有的自动化牲畜监测通常将行为视为孤立事件，忽略了互动关系形成的网络结构。作者希望超越孤立事件视角，建立能区分亲和行为与对抗行为的网络级表征，以更全面地理解动物社会动态。

Method: 采用基于姿态的计算机视觉流水线，对来自一个商业奶牛场挤奶前区域7小时39分钟连续视频进行分析。经过质量控制后，保留1,414个候选互动中的1,183个，涉及36头奶牛和177个二元组合。在预测类别平衡的198个流水线检测片段审计中，自动与人工标注的一致率为82.8%，未加权审计样本宏F1为0.872。将事件按预测价值分为亲和与对抗两个网络层，进行网络密度、传递性、平均路径长度、社群检测（模块度Q）和中心性分析。

Result: 聚合网络连通（密度=0.281，传递性=0.513，平均路径长度=1.88），预测的亲和行为形成五个算法社群（模块度Q=0.429）。观察区域内预测对抗互动占保留事件的72.4%和互动时长的76.0%。伙伴最多的奶牛并非介数中心性最高的个体。按预测价值分离后，亲和层与对抗层在边集、社群划分和个体位置上呈现描述性差异。

Conclusion: 聚合互动计数可能掩盖观察网络的行为构成，价值感知分析为研究竞争、亲和及福利相关变化的假设提供了框架，但在作为福利或健康指标使用前需要纵向验证。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [78] [Catching the Rug: Early Prediction of Fraudulent Memecoins on Solana via Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20271)
*Jianghai Li,Pavel Kuznetsov,Yury Yanovich,Konstantin Nott-Whaley,Igor Vodolazov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次在Solana生态中开展大规模Rug Pull早期检测研究，构建了640万代币、7个月的数据集，发现绝大多数模因币在发布一小时内即呈现Rug Pull特征。仅使用交易前5分钟数据，XGBoost即能实现稳健检测，且多源数据融合（PumpFun与Raydium跨平台）可显著缓解领域偏移、提升检测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模因币在区块链平台上的快速激增增加了欺诈（尤其是Rug Pull）的风险。此前研究聚焦以太坊，而Solana已成为模因币交易量和代币数量领先的区块链，但其Rug Pull机制（以流动性操纵和社会动态为主而非智能合约后门）尚未得到系统研究，亟需大规模早期检测框架以保护投资者。

Method: 构建了包含640万代币、跨越7个月的Solana生态大规模数据集；通过市场分析揭示Rug Pull的时间分布特征；在缺少代码级特征的情况下，仅利用前5分钟交易数据训练经典机器学习模型（特别是XGBoost梯度提升）进行检测；并评估PumpFun与Raydium两平台间的跨平台泛化能力，采用多源数据融合缓解领域偏移。

Result: 绝大多数模因币在发布一小时内即表现出Rug Pull特征；XGBoost在仅使用前5分钟交易数据的情况下即能实现稳健的Rug Pull检测性能；多源数据融合显著缓解了跨平台领域偏移问题，提高了检测的可靠性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了对高吞吐链上DeFi欺诈的理解，证明了即使没有智能合约代码级特征，基于早期交易数据的机器学习方法也能有效检测Rug Pull，并提供了跨平台多源融合的实用框架，为投资者保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of memecoins on blockchain platforms has increased the risk of fraudulent activities, particularly rug pulls. While previous studies have focused on Ethereum-based tokens, this paper shifts the spotlight to Solana, the leading blockchain for memecoins by trading volume and token count. Unlike Ethereum, where rug pulls often exploit smart contract backdoors, Solana memecoin rug pulls are predominantly driven by liquidity manipulation and social dynamics. This research pioneers large-scale rug pull early detection in the Solana ecosystem by assembling a dataset of 6.4 million tokens over 7 months. Market analysis reveals that a vast majority of these memecoins exhibit rug pull characteristics within one hour of launch, highlighting the urgency of short-horizon prediction. Despite the absence of code-level features, we demonstrate that classic machine learning models, particularly Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), achieve robust performance in detecting potential rug pulls using only the first 5 minutes of trading data. Furthermore, we evaluate cross-platform generalization between PumpFun and Raydium, revealing that multi-source data fusion significantly mitigates domain shift and improves detection reliability. This study advances the understanding of DeFi fraud on high-throughput chains and provides a practical framework for protecting investors.

</details>


### [79] [Air Traffic Control Using Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering, Architecture, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19299)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Matthias Casanova,Jordan Kam,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei,Torsten Darrell,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验评估大型语言模型（LLM）能否生成操作上真实的空中交通管制（ATC）通信。基于旧金山“Bay Tour”航线的实验通用航空航班人工转写作为基准（P0），通过飞行员参与的流程设计五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），嵌入有状态的多轮对话管线中。在九个开源和闭源LLM上，变化提示、上下文示例及历史条件方式，用词汇、结构和语义相似度指标及经人类专家验证的LLM-as-judge（GPT-5.5）评分。结果表明：提供工作转写示例可提升相似度，但收紧提示反而无益——最轻量提示效果最佳，最重脚本化提示因自身错误在对话中积累而崩溃，注入正确历史可修复该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATC通信是对安全至关重要的对话，即使空中交通管理的其他部分已半自动化，ATC仍主要由人类驱动。作者旨在实验性地探究LLM能否生成操作真实的ATC传输，以探索LLM辅助ATC的可行路径。

Method: 采用旧金山Bay Tour航线的实验飞行人工转写为基准（P0），通过飞行员参与式流程设计P1-P5五种约束递增的提示结构，嵌入有状态多轮管线中（模型扮演ATC，配合固定飞行员转写，基于累积对话历史）。在九个开源与闭源LLM上，系统性变化提示结构、是否提供不同实验航班的工作转写作为上下文示例，以及模型是条件化自身先前回复还是注入的真实历史。评分采用词汇、结构、语义相似度指标，并结合经人类专家标注验证的LLM-as-judge（GPT-5.5）进行评价。

Result: 提供工作示例能提升输出与基准的相似度；但收紧提示并未带来改进，最轻量提示表现最佳；最重度脚本化的提示因自身错误随对话累积而性能崩溃，而注入正确的历史信息可以修复这一崩溃。该结论在九个模型上保持一致。

Conclusion: 研究勾勒出LLM辅助ATC的具体路径及其当前局限：轻量提示加上下文示例是更优策略，过度脚本化与自回归错误累积是主要瓶颈。工作为未来构建可靠的LLM辅助ATC系统提供了实证基础与方向指引。

Abstract: Air traffic control (ATC) communication is a safety-critical dialogue that remains largely human-driven even as other parts of air traffic management have been semi-automated. In this article, we experimentally evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate operationally realistic ATC transmissions. An experimental general-aviation flight flying over the San Francisco "Bay Tour" route is hand-transcribed and used as ground truth (P0). Through a pilot-in-the-loop process we design five prompt structures (P1-P5) of increasing constraint and embed them in a stateful multi-turn pipeline, where the model plays ATC to a fixed pilot transcript while conditioning on the accumulating dialogue history. Across nine open- and closed-source LLMs we vary the prompt, the presence of a worked transcript from a different experimental flight as an in-context example, and whether the model conditions on its own prior replies or on injected ground-truth history. Turns are scored with lexical, structural, and semantic similarity metrics and by an LLM-as-judge (GPT-5.5) validated against human expert annotation. Supplying a worked example improves similarity, but tightening the prompt does not: the lightest prompts perform best and the most heavily scripted one collapses as its own errors accumulate through the dialogue, which injecting correct history repairs. These results outline a concrete path and its current limits toward LLM-assisted ATC.

</details>


### [80] [Outcome Monitors: Recovery Affordances for Silent Tool Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19303)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Outcome Monitors（结果监视器），用于检测工具调用超时或返回错误但格式正确的"结果契约"违规。监视器在违规时保留结果并发出附带恢复工具的非约束性回执，在ToolMaze上使完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%，在tau-bench零售两档分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点；消融实验表明恢复工具列表是关键的生效成分，而检测词汇之外的扩展检测仍有待突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用超时时智能体能感知失败并绕行，但缓存错误页面或负价格等故障可以以预期格式返回并被当作事实消费，造成静默失败。现有系统缺乏对此类"格式正确但内容违规"结果的检测机制。

Method: 提出Outcome Monitors，从任务不相交的轨迹中挖掘或以公共模式推断"结果契约"（outcome contracts），检测结果是否违反契约。一旦发现违规，监视器保留原始结果并发出非约束性回执（receipt），其中指明违规属性及公开恢复工具。

Result: 在冻结的预设评估中注入故障后：ToolMaze完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%（覆盖两家供应商家族的四个模型，并在第三家复现）；tau-bench零售两档分别提升14.0和12.0个点。消融实验显示移除恢复工具列表后增益消失，恢复后效果重现；诊断细节与时机无显著影响。在从公开事件分类法转录的套件上，词汇外检测率降至46%，但投递与完成率不变。

Conclusion: Outcome Monitors能有效提升智能体在静默失败场景下的任务完成率，恢复工具是回执中起效的核心成分；增益集中在故障阻断完成的场景。当前检测仍依赖契约词汇表，扩展检测到词汇之外的范围是未来工作方向。

Abstract: When a tool call times out, the agent sees the failure and can route around it. A cached error page or negative price can instead arrive in the expected format and be consumed as fact. We introduce Outcome Monitors, which detect violations of outcome contracts mined from task-disjoint traces or derived from public schemas. On a violation, the monitor preserves the result and issues a nonbinding receipt naming the violated property and public recovery tools. In frozen, prespecified evaluations with injected failures, Outcome Monitors raise ToolMaze completion from 10.9% to 28.1% across four models in two provider families and replicate in a third. In tau-bench retail, completion improves by 14.0 and 12.0 points on two tiers. In separate ToolMaze controls, removing the recovery-tool list eliminates the measured gain and restoring it recovers the effect; diagnostic detail and timing produce no detectable differences. Gains concentrate where the fault blocks completion. On a suite transcribed from a published incident taxonomy, detection outside the mined vocabulary falls to 46%, though delivery continues and completion is unchanged. Recovery tools are the active receipt content in these controls; extending detection beyond the contract vocabulary remains open.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Imitation: Filtering On-Policy Distillation by Reasoning Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19408)
*Chen Yang,Haiyuan Wan,Rengrong Xiong,Yize Chen,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出R2-OPD方法，通过构建教师奖励与独立推理进度奖励两个轨迹内排名，在两者不一致时选择性抑制蒸馏奖励，以解决on-policy distillation中教师奖励与真实推理进度冲突的问题，从而提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy distillation (OPD)框架隐式假设教师奖励是推理进度的合适代理，因此在策略优化过程中对所有教师反馈一视同仁。然而实际中该假设往往不成立：推理步骤即使有明显进步，也可能因偏离教师输出而获得较低的蒸馏奖励，导致教师奖励与真实推理进度冲突，从而误导策略优化。

Method: 提出Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD)。该方法构建两个轨迹内推理跨度排名：一个来自教师蒸馏奖励，另一个来自独立估计的推理进度奖励。当两个排名不一致时，选择性抑制蒸馏奖励，从而减少与推理进度冲突的监督信号，同时保留有效的教师指导。

Result: 实验表明，R2-OPD相较于标准OPD在推理性能上持续取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 通过奖励过滤机制缓解教师奖励与真实推理进度之间的不对齐，R2-OPD能更有效地利用教师信号，提升语言模型蒸馏后的推理能力。该方法验证了在蒸馏过程中显式建模推理进度的重要性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective framework for post-training language models by pairing student-generated trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. However, OPD implicitly assumes that teacher-derived rewards are an appropriate proxy for reasoning progress, and therefore treats all teacher feedback equally during policy optimization. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. To address this mismatch, we propose Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD), which constructs two within-trajectory rankings of reasoning spans, one from teacher-derived rewards and the other from independently estimated progress reward. Distillation rewards are selectively suppressed whenever the two rankings disagree, reducing supervision that conflicts with reasoning progress while preserving effective teacher guidance. Our approach shows consistent improvement over standard OPD especially regarding reasoning performances.

</details>


### [82] [Symposium: Trust via Auditable Records for Communities of AI Scientist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19511)
*Dexter Pratt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Symposium是一个形式化框架和实用实现，用于记录小型科学研究社区中AI智能体的操作，提供长期不可变的代理驱动研究活动历史，包括可审计的分析、假设、数据和科学论述痕迹，使智能体能够基于先前工作构建并支持目的相关的信任评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究社区部署多样化的AI系统，需要一个独立的框架将社区持久的科学历史与在历史上运行的智能体及其他系统分离，以保留可审计的证据和信任评估的基础。

Method: Symposium作为发布基础设施实现，捕获科学论证内容，包括结构化声明、细粒度证据引用、假设和明确的证据使用声明；提供智能体提示组件和文档，使社区能快速搭建并运行自己的Symposium社区。其核心设计原则是将社区持久历史与具体AI智能体系统解耦。

Result: 提供了一个可工作的实现，包括发布基础设施、智能体提示组件和文档，能够支持社区快速部署；提供长期不可见改变的历史记录，使智能体能够基于先前发表的人工产物继续工作。

Conclusion: Symposium与AI协同科学智能体或集成式AI研究环境不同，它是一个独立框架，通过分离科学社区的历史记录与操作这些记录的系统，适应快速演化的多样化AI环境，为小型科学研究社区提供可审计、可信任的研究记录基础设施。

Abstract: Symposium is a formal framework and practical implementation to record the operation of AI agents deployed by small scientific research communities. Symposium provides long-term, immutable histories of agent-driven research activity, leaving auditable trails of analyses, hypotheses, data, and scientific discourse. This shared record of published artifacts enables agents to build on prior work and preserves the evidence researchers and agents need to make purpose-dependent trust assessments. Symposium captures scientific argument, including structured claims, fine-grained evidence citations, assumptions, and explicit declarations of what material may and may not be used as evidence. Symposium differs from AI co-scientist agents or integrated AI research environments; it is a framework that separates a scientific community's durable history from the agents and other systems that operate on that history. It assumes that a community will use diverse AI systems in a rapidly evolving environment. A working implementation of the publication infrastructure, agent prompt components, and documentation are provided to enable users to rapidly set up and run their own Symposium community.

</details>


### [83] [Enforcing LLM Safety through DMD-based Classification of Prompt-Response Embedding Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19579)
*Mohamed Akrout,Olivera Kotevska,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于动力系统框架的黑盒LLM安全分类方法，通过将提示词和响应投射到高维嵌入空间，为安全与不安全两种状态分别拟合Koopman预测模型，利用差分残差分数进行分类。该方法在三个安全基准上评估，结果表明融合提示词嵌入能带来一致的改进，特别是在交互依赖型违规场景中效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险场景中部署日益增多，但其产生有毒、有害或违反政策内容的风险显著。现有的黑盒安全检测方法效率不足，且缺乏能够捕捉提示词与响应之间交互模式的系统性方法。作者希望弥合这一空白，并探索利用动力系统理论来分析AI系统本身。

Method: 将提示词和响应分别嵌入高维向量空间，为安全与非安全两个状态各自拟合基于Koopman算子的预测模型；提出一个新的差分残差分数（differential residual score），通过比较安全与不安全状态的预测误差来对新输出进行分类。关键创新在于同时纳入提示词与响应的嵌入动态，使拟合的Koopman算子能够捕捉两者的交互模式。

Result: 在三个安全基准测试中使用三种嵌入模型进行评估，结果表明：融合提示词嵌入一致地提升了分类性能，特别是对于交互依赖型违规，搭配因果解码器（如Llama-3）时效果最佳；而仅依赖响应的违规则更适合使用稠密语义嵌入表示。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了动力系统框架在LLM安全分类中的有效性，开启了利用动力系统分析AI系统这一新的研究范式，而非传统的"用AI建模动力系统"的主导范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications, yet their tendency to generate toxic, harmful, or policy-violating content poses significant risks. Detecting these unsafe outputs efficiently in a black-box manner remains an open challenge. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed dynamical systems framework designed for hallucination detection to LLM safety classification. By projecting both prompts and responses into high-dimensional embedding spaces and fitting separate Koopman-based predictive models for safe and unsafe regimes, we classify new outputs using a new differential residual score that compares prediction errors of the safe and unsafe regimes. A key contribution is the incorporation of the prompt and response embedding dynamics, yielding fitted Koopman operators that capture crucial interaction patterns. We evaluate our black-box method across three safety benchmarks using three embedding models. Our results show that incorporating prompt embeddings yields consistent improvements, particularly for interaction-dependent violations when paired with causal decoders (e.g., in Llama-3), while response-only violations benefit more from dense semantic embedding representations. These findings opens the door for using dynamical systems to analyze AI systems rather than the dominant paradigm of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [84] [Scientific Data Skills: Enabling Agent-Ready Scientific Data Services at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19625)
*Xiaohan Huang,Qingqing Long,Xiaolei Du,Siyu Pu,Jiawen Xu,Haotian Chen,Chenyang Zhao,Jinbiao Liu,Xuezhi Wang,Hao Wang,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出科学数据技能（SciDSK），一种面向AI智能体的数据集表示方法，将数据集特定知识与操作指南打包为可复用的智能体技能，并在六个科学学科中发布，实验表明其能显著提升智能体驱动的数据集发现与解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据日益被AI智能体使用，但现有的数据集表示对自主发现、解释和调用支持有限。这种局限性源于科学数据在异构存储库中的碎片化，以及现有数据集表示主要为人类使用而设计。

Method: 引入SciDSK表示，集成数据集描述、科学背景、文件组织、使用流程、质量检查和来源信息，同时保留原始数据在存储库中。定义了结构化SciDSK规范，开发了系统性构建流水线，将每个SciDSK锚定在权威数据集记录及配套材料上。建立科学数据技能库（Scientific Data Skill Bank）平台，横跨六个科学学科发布资源，支持包访问、持久标识和源数据追踪。通过检索基准和受控案例分别评估数据集发现和解释能力。

Result: SciDSK显著改善了智能体驱动的数据集发现，并为数据集解释提供了更精确、更可操作的支持。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将数据集特定知识组织成智能体就绪表示的价值，表明SciDSK是一种有效的数据集表示方案。

Abstract: Scientific data are increasingly used by AI agents, yet existing dataset representations provide limited support for autonomous discovery, interpretation, and invocation. This limitation stems from the fragmentation of scientific data across heterogeneous repositories and from dataset representations designed primarily for human use. To address this limitation, we introduce the Scientific Data Skill (SciDSK), an agent-ready representation that packages dataset-specific knowledge and operational guidance as a reusable agent skill. A SciDSK integrates dataset descriptions, scientific context, file organization, usage procedures, quality checks, and provenance information while retaining the underlying data in its original repository. We define a structured SciDSK specification and develop a systematic construction pipeline that grounds each SciDSK in authoritative dataset records and associated supporting materials. We further establish the Scientific Data Skill Bank, a unified platform that publishes SciDSK resources across six scientific disciplines and supports package access, persistent identification, and traceability to source datasets. We evaluate SciDSK through a retrieval benchmark for dataset discovery and controlled cases for dataset interpretation. The results show that SciDSK improves agent-driven dataset discovery and provides more precise and actionable support for dataset interpretation. These findings support the value of organizing dataset-specific knowledge in an agent-ready representation.

</details>


### [85] [Can Agent Memory Systems Track Evolving State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19652)
*Xinyi Fan,Miri Liu,Ruozhen Yang,Siru Ouyang,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出状态追踪（state tracking）这一LLM代理记忆系统的关键能力维度，构建了包含234个多会话场景的StateMemBench基准，并提出StateMem状态优先记忆方法，在多个后端上将当前状态准确率提升1.6-1.8倍，且可作为轻量级包装器为现有记忆系统带来+32至+67点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理记忆基准主要聚焦于回忆类任务，忽视了代理在长时间、高风险任务中需要追踪世界演化的状态——事实、约束和决策在交互中被不断修订，答案必须反映当前状态而非被取代的旧状态。这一能力缺口尚未被现有基准和记忆系统有效覆盖。

Method: 1) 定义状态追踪能力并构建StateMemBench，包含234个多会话场景、两种对话长度区间，采用封闭池评分区分当前状态、被取代状态和失败答案；2) 提出StateMem，一种状态优先的记忆方法，显式追踪状态取代关系和关系依赖；3) 将状态方法作为轻量级单调用包装器应用于现有记忆系统。

Result: StateMem在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上比最强同骨干基线提升1.8倍当前状态准确率（0.205→0.363），在Qwen-3.5-9B上比最强记忆系统提升1.6倍（0.149→0.233），同时与长上下文基线保持竞争力；作为包装器在六个记忆和检索后端上带来+32至+67点提升，其中控制实验表明+15至+32点归因于状态结构本身。

Conclusion: 状态追踪是记忆系统独立于回忆能力的关键维度，现有系统在此任务上表现不佳；StateMem证明了显式状态建模的有效性，且其状态方法可泛化为通用增强手段，显著提升现有记忆系统的当前状态准确性。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents are deployed for longer and higher-stakes tasks, their memory systems continue to have crucial gaps. While existing memory benchmarks focus largely on recall-shaped tasks, we argue an effective memory system must track the evolving state of the world; as facts, constraints, and decisions are revised over a long interaction, answers must reflect the current state and not a superseded one. We define this capability as state tracking and instantiate it in StateMemBench, a benchmark of 234 multi-session scenarios spanning two conversation-length regimes. Its closed-pool grading scores whether an answer reflects the current state, the superseded state, or fails otherwise, separating state-tracking failures from other errors by construction. Our analysis shows that this task is challenging for existing memory systems, retrieval-augmented baselines, and long-context baselines. We then present StateMem, a state-first memory method that explicitly tracks supersession and relational dependencies, and show it improves current-state accuracy over the strongest same-backbone baseline by 1.8x (0.205 -> 0.363) on DeepSeek-V4-Flash and over the strongest memory system by 1.6x (0.149 -> 0.233) on Qwen-3.5-9B, while remaining competitive with the long-context baselines. Finally, we show the same state approach can be applied as a lightweight single-call wrapper over existing memory systems, lifting current-state accuracy by +32 to +67 points on StateMemBench across six memory and retrieval backends. A length- and cost-matched control attributes +15 to +32 of those points to state structure rather than added context.

</details>


### [86] [Frequency-Aware Continual Learning for Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19680)
*Tenghui Huang,Jiawen Kang,Dongning Liu,Changyan Yi,Chengjun Cai,Anjia Yang,Li Li,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段智能合约漏洞检测框架，应对LLM适应新漏洞类别、灾难性遗忘和适配器合并三个因果关联的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约漏洞检测中，新漏洞类别不断出现需要参数高效适应，但全量重训练成本高昂；按任务训练适配器会导致灾难性遗忘；多个适配器因推理时任务身份未知而必须合并为单一模型。这三个挑战因果相连，需要集成式框架解决。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) FA-LoRA在傅里叶域进行频率感知的低秩适应，仅需0.4%可训练参数；(2) FAR利用频率门控通过损失动态估计逐样本遗忘风险并优先回放脆弱知识；(3) APPM利用FAR产生的非对称泛化识别最强泛化适配器作为锚点，通过锚点保护加权合并和频域门控竞争将全部适配器合并为单一模型。

Result: FA-LoRA优于标准LoRA和QLoRA；FAR在顺序任务上平均Micro-F1达0.8022；APPM达到Micro-F1 0.8085，与独立逐任务上界仅差2.7%，合并耗时156ms且无额外运行时内存开销。

Conclusion: 在DIVE实验上验证了该框架能有效应对区块链生态演化中智能合约漏洞检测的三个关联挑战。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerability detection with Large Language Models (LLMs) faces three causally linked challenges. First, new vulnerability categories demand parameter-efficient adaptation, since full retraining is prohibitive for sequentially arriving tasks. Second, training per-task adapters on a shared backbone causes catastrophic forgetting of previously learned vulnerabilities. Third, the resulting multiplicity of adapters must be consolidated into a single model, since task identity is unknown at inference time. Each challenge arises directly from the solution to its predecessor, making an integrated framework essential. We propose a three-stage pipeline in which each stage addresses one challenge and feeds into the next. The adaptation stage uses Frequency-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation (FA-LoRA), which performs adaptation in the Fourier domain with per-frequency importance gates, requiring only 0.4% trainable parameters while outperforming standard LoRA and QLoRA. The continual learning stage applies Forget-Aware Replay (FAR), which uses these frequency gates to estimate per-sample forgetting risk via loss dynamics and prioritizes vulnerable knowledge for rehearsal, achieving an average Micro-F1 of 0.8022 across sequential tasks. The deployment stage employs Anchor-Protected Progressive Merging (APPM), which exploits the asymmetric generalization produced by FAR training to identify the strongest-generalizing adapter as an anchor and consolidates all adapters into a single model via anchor-protected weighted merging with frequency-domain gate competition. APPM achieves a Micro-F1 of 0.8085, within 2.7% of the independent per-task upper bound, at a merge cost of 156 ms and no additional runtime memory. Experiments on DIVE confirm the framework effectively addresses all three challenges for evolving blockchain ecosystems.

</details>


### [87] [Learning Hierarchical Skill Policies with Offline Quality-Diversity Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19684)
*Tanachai Anakewat,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QDOS（Quality-Diversity Offline Skill learning），一种统一的离线到在线强化学习流水线，通过优势加权质量-多样性预训练目标提取多样化且高价值的技能，并结合双数据集复用策略，显著提升稀疏奖励任务中的策略性能和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段策略（先从未标记数据集提取低层技能，再训练高层策略）中，低层策略的质量高度依赖于数据集质量，导致技能提取不够鲁棒，无法保证提取到高价值且多样化的技能。

Method: 1) 提出Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity预训练目标，用轨迹片段的估计优势对技能提取和多样性目标进行加权，以提取多样且高价值的技能；2) 采用双数据集复用策略，离线数据既用于技能预训练，又通过伪标签方式填充在线回放缓冲区。

Result: 在结构化操作任务和非结构化运动任务上，QDOS显著优于强基线方法，验证了其在稀疏奖励域中加速探索和提升最终回报的能力。

Conclusion: 通过优势加权质量-多样性预训练和双数据集复用，QDOS有效解决了离线技能提取对数据集质量的依赖问题，为离线到在线强化学习提供了更鲁棒的技能表示。

Abstract: Recent studies investigate how to leverage pre-collected datasets to improve the policy performance and sample efficiency of RL. One promising approach to achieve this goal is to employ a two-stage strategy: In the first stage, diverse skills are extracted as a low-level policy from a given dataset, and a high-level policy is trained to solve a specific task in the second stage. Typically, extraction of the low-level policy is performed based on unsupervised learning such as trajectory VAE. However, a limitation of this approach is that the quality of the low-level policy highly depends on the quality of the dataset. To address this issue, we introduce QDOS (Quality-Diversity Offline Skill learning), a unified pipeline for robust offline-to-online learning. Our approach incorporates an Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity pretraining objective, which weights the skill extraction and diversity objectives by the estimated advantage of each trajectory segment. This approach allows the model to extract diverse and high-value skills. By providing robust and task-relevant skill representations, QDOS significantly improves the quality of the embedded skill space used by the low-level policy. We further integrate this with a dual dataset reuse strategy, where offline data is used both for skill pretraining and for populating the online replay buffer via pseudo-labeling. Experiments demonstrate that QDOS significantly outperforms strong baselines in structured manipulation tasks and unstructured locomotion tasks, confirming its ability to accelerate exploration and improve final returns in challenging sparse-reward domains.

</details>


### [88] [Rethinking the Evaluation and Optimization of LLM-Based Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19689)
*Pei Wang,Xu Chen,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出主观性系数（subjectivity coefficient）来量化人类行为的主观性程度，并据此提出主观性自适应软标签训练方法（SALT），通过语义相近输入的输出池化生成软分布标签，解决LLM社会模拟中基于准确率的评估与硬标签训练在主观任务上的失效问题。同时构建了包含19,300个上下文、193位标注者和100个主观问题的SUBJSIM基准，用于在仅有单次观察数据的情况下训练模型并评估其与完整响应分布的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM社会模拟是传统调查和行为实验的有力补充，但现有评估方式通过准确率检验模型是否选出人类观察到的单一响应，训练方式也仅让LLM复现硬标签。然而人类行为本质上是主观的，同一人在同一情境下可能合理地产出不同行为，单一观察值只是潜在响应分布中的一次抽样，导致基于准确率的评估不可靠、硬标签训练具有误导性。

Method: 1) 定义基于熵的主观性系数，用于区分客观任务（如编码）与主观任务（如社会模拟），并系统分析准确率评估和硬标签训练随主观性增长而失效的方式。2) 提出SALT（Subjectivity-Adaptive soft-Label Training），将语义相近输入的观察输出汇聚为软分布标签，聚合半径随每个输入估计的主观性自适应调整；在近客观极限下邻域收缩，自然退化为标准单标签训练。3) 构建SUBJSIM基准，包含19,300个上下文、193位标注者和100个主观问题，弥补现有数据集仅记录单一观察响应、无法支撑分布评估的不足。

Result: 在SUBJSIM上的实验表明，在真实场景中（现实数据通常每个输入仅有一次观察），模型从单一观察输出训练而针对完整响应分布进行评估，SALT方法展现出优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: 主观性程度应当被显式建模，软标签训练结合主观性自适应的聚合半径可以提升LLM社会模拟的人类行为保真度，该框架为可扩展的分布级评估与训练提供了新路径。

Abstract: LLM-based social simulation is a promising complement to traditional methods such as surveys and behavioral experiments. A core question is how to evaluate the fidelity of LLM-simulated human behavior and optimize LLMs toward it. Prevailing practice evaluates by accuracy, checking whether the model selects the single response observed from a human, and trains the LLM to reproduce this hard label. However, human behavior is inherently subjective: the same person in the same situation may reasonably act differently, so an observed response is only one draw from an underlying response distribution, rendering accuracy-based evaluation unreliable and hard-label training misleading. To address these problems, we first introduce the subjectivity coefficient, an entropy-based quantity distinguishing objective tasks such as coding from subjective ones such as social simulation, and use it to systematically analyze how accuracy-based evaluation and hard-label training fail as subjectivity grows. Based on the subjectivity coefficient, we propose Subjectivity-Adaptive soft-Label Training (SALT): it pools observed outputs from semantically nearby inputs into soft distributional labels, with an aggregation radius adapted to the estimated subjectivity of each input; in the near-objective limit the neighborhood shrinks, so SALT naturally falls back to standard single-label training. Moreover, since existing datasets record only single observed responses and cannot support distributional evaluation, we construct SUBJSIM, a benchmark of 19,300 contexts covering 193 annotators and 100 subjective questions. Since real-world data typically provide only a single observation per input, our experiments train models from single observed outputs while evaluating them against the full response distributions, verifying feasibility in realistic settings. Results on SUBJSIM demonstrate the advantages of our method.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Memory Majority: Latent-Source Reasoning for Multi-Agent Memory Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19701)
*Chenchen Lin,Wenhao Yuan,Xuehe Wang,Edith Cheuk Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAMA框架，解决多智能体长期记忆中记忆相关性偏差问题——即不同智能体写的记忆可能源于同一上游来源或共享偏见，导致相关证据被重复计数形成虚假多数。CAMA通过解耦检索到的记忆并恢复缺失的独立证据来实现可靠仲裁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体记忆方法将检索到的记忆视为独立证据，通过投票或加权组合。但在多智能体场景中，不同智能体写入的记忆可能继承同一上游来源或共享偏差，导致相关证据被重复计数，形成虚假多数。这一"记忆相关性偏差"（Memory Correlation Bias）问题在现有工作中未被解决。

Method: CAMA框架包括两个关键组件：(1) 将检索到的记忆建模为查询条件证据组，结合神经网络依赖推断与基于来源（provenance）的符号先验来估计有效独立证据源的数量，从而防止相关记忆形成虚假多数；(2) 由于关键独立证据可能不在初始检索集合中，学习一个序贯恢复策略，主动检索替代证据或追溯上游来源，在最小化检索成本的同时恢复足够的独立证据，最终做出仲裁决策。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，CAMA优于最先进的基线方法，有效抑制了由相关记忆诱导的虚假多数现象。

Conclusion: CAMA通过联合解耦相关记忆并主动恢复缺失的独立证据，为解决多智能体长期记忆系统中的证据相关性问题提供了有效方案，在提升仲裁可靠性的同时控制了检索成本。

Abstract: Long-term multi-agent systems continuously accumulate the memories produced by different agents. Existing memory methods typically treat retrieved memories as independent evidence and combine them through voting or weighting. However, this independence assumption often fails in multi-agent settings: memories written by different agents may inherit the same upstream source or shared bias, causing correlated evidence to be repeatedly counted and creating a false majority. We term this failure mode \textit{Memory Correlation Bias}. To address the issue, we propose the \textbf{C}orrelation-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{M}emory \textbf{A}rbitration (CAMA) framework that jointly decouples retrieved memories and recovers missing independent evidence. We model the retrieved memories as query-conditioned evidence groups and combine neural dependency inference with provenance-based symbolic priors to estimate the effective number of independent evidence sources, thereby preventing correlated memories from forming a false majority. Since critical independent evidence may be absent from the initial retrieval set, \textsc{CAMA} further learns a sequential recovery policy that actively retrieves alternative evidence or traces upstream sources before making the final decision, aiming to recover sufficient independent evidence for reliable arbitration while minimizing retrieval cost. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our method over the state-of-the-art baseline methods, suppressing false majorities induced by correlated memories.

</details>


### [90] [SafeBranch: Branch-Pair Safety Alignment for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19729)
*Hyunse Lee,Jiwoo Jeong,Haneul Lee,Kyochul Jang,Youngjae Yu,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeBranch是一个通过环境回滚构建"分支对"来对齐具身智能体安全行为的框架。它将不安全轨迹回滚到导致违规的关键步骤，查询智能体获得安全替代动作，使两个分支仅在该步骤有差异，从而在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下大幅提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体在执行指令任务时虽能完成任务但常违反安全约束。训练安全问题困难在于：安全与任务成功是不同目标，且安全只出现在轨迹中少数关键步骤。标准监督不足——模仿安全轨迹无法解释其安全原因；对比任意安全/不安全轨迹会混淆安全信号与无关差异。

Method: SafeBranch框架：(1) 从智能体自身的不安全rollout中通过环境回滚定位导致违规的安全关键步骤；(2) 在该步骤查询智能体获取安全替代动作；(3) 将原动作与替代动作配对为"分支对"，使两个分支仅在该关键步骤不同，从而隔离安全信号与其他差异，用于训练对齐。

Result: 在IS-Bench、SafetyALFRED及其包含未见任务和物体的分布外变体上，SafeBranch能可靠地处理安全性而不牺牲任务成功率，在未见物体变体上比未训练基线实现约十倍的安全成功次数。

Conclusion: SafeBranch通过构造仅在安全关键步骤有差异的分支对，有效解耦了安全信号与无关轨迹差异，使具身智能体在部署时无需critic即可安全行动，兼顾安全性与任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-model-based embodied agents can complete instructed tasks but often violate safety constraints in the process, a problem recently framed as interactive safety. Training such agents to act safely is difficult, since safety and task success are distinct objectives, and safety arises only at a small number of safety-critical steps within a trajectory. Standard supervision is insufficient: imitating safe trajectories teaches behavior without explaining why it is safe, and contrasting arbitrary safe and unsafe trajectories mixes the safety signal with unrelated differences. We propose SafeBranch, a framework that aligns an embodied actor on safety through branch pairs constructed from the actor's own unsafe rollouts via environment rollback. SafeBranch rolls each unsafe rollout back to the safety-critical step that caused the violation, queries the actor for a safe alternative, and pairs the original action with the alternative so that the two branches differ only at that step. The trained actor acts safely at deployment with no critic in the loop. On IS-Bench, SafetyALFRED, and out-of-distribution variants with unseen tasks and objects, it handles safety reliably without sacrificing task success, achieving roughly ten times more safe successes than the untrained baseline on the unseen-object variant.

</details>


### [91] [GenMatch: An End-to-End Generative Matching Framework for Micro-View Order-Dispatching in Ride-Hailing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19751)
*Chuang Liu,Yuxueqing Zhang,Tengfei Lyu,Zirui Yuan,Weiqi Hu,Yanghan Cheng,Ming Wang,Li Ma,Zihao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenMatch是首个部署于真实生产环境的端到端生成式匹配框架，用于网约车平台微观视角的订单派发。它将订单派发建模为生成式匹配问题，通过上下文感知二部图编码器、业务感知效用学习器和状态感知指针解码器，解决多阶段范式下跨阶段目标不一致的问题，在滴滴五个国际市场的离线评估和在线A/B测试中均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流工业方案采用"模型预测-价值计算-派单匹配"的多阶段范式，但各阶段优化的是不同的中间目标，导致跨阶段目标不一致，即改进单一阶段不一定能提升最终的整体派单效果。因此需要端到端地优化最终批量级分配目标。

Method: 将微观订单派发建模为生成式匹配问题，提出GenMatch框架，包含三个核心组件：1) Context-Aware Bipartite Encoder用于高效编码动态稀疏二部图；2) Business-Aware Utility Learner从异构反馈中学习统一的业务效用函数，替代手工设计的价值函数；3) State-Aware Pointer Decoder跟踪演化中的匹配状态，逐对生成订单-司机的分配结果。

Result: 在滴滴国际网约车五个城市的离线评估和在线A/B测试中，GenMatch相比竞争性基线取得了一致的性能提升，验证了其在工业订单派发中的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: GenMatch作为首个部署于生产环境的端到端生成式匹配框架，成功解决了多阶段范式的目标不一致问题，证明了生成式建模在订单派发这一工业关键场景中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Micro-View Order-Dispatching assigns available drivers to passenger orders within each dispatch batch and is critical to the service quality and operational efficiency of ride-hailing platforms. Mainstream industrial solutions follow a multi-stage paradigm of model prediction, value calculation, and dispatch matching. Although dispatch quality is determined by the final batch-level assignment, these stages optimize different intermediate objectives. This cross-stage objective inconsistency means that improving a single stage does not necessarily improve the overall dispatch result. We therefore formulate Micro-View Order-Dispatching as a generative matching problem and propose GenMatch, an end-to-end Generative Matching framework and the first such framework deployed in a real-world production environment. Applying generative modeling to this problem introduces three challenges. First, each dispatch batch forms a dynamic sparse bipartite graph, requiring efficient structured batch-level encoding. Second, replacing the hand-crafted value function requires learning unified business utility from heterogeneous feedback. Third, directly generating an assignment requires tracking the evolving matching state because each selected order-driver pair changes the remaining feasible candidates. GenMatch addresses these challenges with a Context-Aware Bipartite Encoder, a Business-Aware Utility Learner, and a State-Aware Pointer Decoder. Extensive offline evaluations and online A/B tests in five cities across DiDi's international ride-hailing markets show consistent improvements over competitive baselines, confirming the effectiveness and practicality of GenMatch for industrial order-dispatching.

</details>


### [92] [TT-net: Quantum Inspired Tensor Network Denoising in Conditional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19789)
*Michal A. Sterzel,Marko J. Rančić*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TT-Net，用双切张量火车（tensor-train）分解替代传统SVD单切分解，用于条件GAN图像去噪。TT-Net能够跨通道访问信息，在受控对比中于PSNR和SSIM指标上全面超越SVD-Net，并在高斯噪声下超越EigenGAN和Pix2pix等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件GAN图像去噪架构（如SVD-Net）使用SVD作为单切分解步骤，仅能逐通道（per-channel）处理生成器特征图，缺乏跨通道信息访问能力。作者假设这种跨通道信息访问能提升去噪质量，因此希望引入量子物理中成熟的张量网络工具来弥补这一局限。

Method: TT-Net将SVD-Net中的逐通道SVD去噪块替换为双切张量火车分解（two-cut tensor-train decomposition）。张量火车（即量子计算社区中的矩阵乘积态MPS）能够直接访问跨通道信息。在受控实验中，两种网络仅在分解机制上存在差异，其余架构保持一致，从而隔离验证跨通道访问的作用。此外通过训练动力学分析观察对抗损失和重建质量的演化情况。

Result: 在三种噪声类型（高斯噪声、运动模糊、椒盐噪声）下，TT-Net在PSNR和SSIM指标上均优于SVD-Net。在高斯噪声下，TT-Net还超越了EigenGAN和当前最先进的Pix2pix方法（后者不采用任何线性代数分解）。训练动力学分析显示，TT-Net的对抗损失项在三种噪声下均持续饱和到停滞状态，且比SVD-Net更明显，但重建质量仍持续改善。

Conclusion: 跨通道信息访问确实能提升图像去噪质量，量子启发的张量网络工具可作为深度学习实际应用的有效特征滤波器。同时，对抗损失饱和但重建质量继续提升的现象引发了一个有待解决的开放问题：对抗成分对去噪质量的真实贡献是什么。

Abstract: Developed as a workhorse for classical simulations of quantum algorithms and quantum many-body systems, Tensor Network methods have entered the scientific mainstream in quantum physics. Among various types of tensor networks, Tensor Trains (commonly know as Matrix Product States in the quantum computing community) have already found applications in machine learning. These methods often rely on a powerful linear algebra tool called the Singular Value Decomposition (SVD). Several conditional GAN architectures for image denoising incorporate SVD as a single-cut decomposition step applied to generator feature maps. In this work we introduce TT-Net, which replaces the per-channel SVD denoising block with a two-cut tensor-train decomposition capable of accessing cross-channel information directly, a capability absent from contemporary alternatives. In a controlled comparison differing only in this decomposition mechanism, TT-Net outperforms SVD-Net on PSNR and SSIM across all three noise types tested (Gaussian, motion blur, and salt-and-pepper), supporting the hypothesis that cross-channel access improves denoising quality. Training-dynamics analysis further shows that TT-Net's adversarial loss term consistently saturates to a stagnant state across all three noise types, more so than SVD-Net's, while reconstruction quality continues to improve regardless, raising an open question about the adversarial component's contribution that this work identifies but does not resolve. Furthermore, for Gaussian noise our method outperforms both the EigenGAN and the state of the art Pix2pix method which does not assume any linear algebra decompositions and does not retain any linear algebra information. Our manuscript shows how quantum inspired tools can be used as practical real world feature filters for deep learning applications.

</details>


### [93] [LLMs as Acquisition Policies for Finite-Pool Materials Optimization: A Controlled Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19790)
*Dino-Rober Demir,Florian Le Bronnec,Rio Yokota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放权重的大型语言模型（LLMs）能否作为独立的采集策略（acquisition policy）用于有限候选池的材料优化任务。研究比较了五种LLM与随机选择和传统高斯过程方法在四种回溯性材料优化任务中的表现，发现LLM策略通常能比随机选择更快达到全局最优，但在多数任务上性能不如高斯过程方法，且性能波动大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料发现通常需要在巨大的候选空间中搜索，而实验或计算评估成本高昂。主动学习通过代理模型选择下一个要评估的候选物来降低这一成本，但传统方法依赖概率代理模型。作者希望探索开放权重LLM是否无需任务特定训练即可直接作为独立的采集策略，从而简化主动学习流程并利用LLM的常识与科学知识。

Method: 在四种回溯性有限池材料优化任务中，系统评估五种开放权重LLM作为采集策略的性能。研究中改变候选呈现方式（candidate-presentation strategies），并将LLM策略与随机选择和传统高斯过程方法进行对比，考察不同任务、模型、初始化和候选呈现方式下的表现。

Result: LLM策略通常比随机选择在更少的迭代次数内达到全局最优，表明其无需任务特定训练即可提供有用的采集信号。但相对于高斯过程方法，表现是混合的：传统采集方法在大多数任务上表现更好，而LLM在某些设置下可与之匹敌或超越。性能在不同任务、模型、初始化和候选呈现方式间差异显著，没有任何一种LLM方法在所有任务中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 开放权重LLM作为有限池材料搜索的采集策略具有一定潜力，但其可靠性对任务类型以及候选物和科学上下文的呈现方式高度敏感，尚不能全面取代传统高斯过程方法，需要进一步研究以提高其稳定性。

Abstract: Discovering materials with desirable properties often requires searching large candidate spaces while experimental or computational evaluations remain costly. Active learning addresses this challenge by using previous observations to select which candidate to evaluate next, typically through probabilistic surrogate models. We investigate whether open-weight large language models (LLMs) can serve as standalone acquisition policies in this setting. We evaluate five LLMs across four retrospective finite-pool materials optimization tasks under different candidate-presentation strategies and compare them with random selection and conventional Gaussian-process methods. LLM policies generally reach the global optimum in fewer iterations than random selection, indicating that they provide a useful acquisition signal without task-specific training. Their performance relative to Gaussian-process methods is mixed: conventional acquisition performs better on most tasks, while LLMs match or outperform it in some settings. Performance varies substantially across tasks, models, initializations, and candidate presentations, with no LLM approach performing best across all tasks. Overall, open-weight LLMs show potential as acquisition policies for finite-pool materials search, although their reliability remains sensitive to the task and to how candidates and scientific context are presented.

</details>


### [94] [Towards general embodied intelligence: integrating large language models, knowledge bases, and reasoning capabilities to build the next generation of AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19794)
*Fujiang Yuan,Xia Huang,Lusheng Wang,Jun Ding,Zhen Tian,Yuxin Wang,Shaojie Gu,Yuki Funabora,Yanhong Peng,Zebing Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大型语言模型、结构化知识库与推理能力三者融合推动通用具身智能的发展路径，提出了一个概念性框架来阐明LLM、KB、RA与具身化之间的协同关系，并识别出五大关键挑战（高效部署、闭环知识集成、混合符号-神经推理、感知-行动接地、持续学习）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在自然语言处理领域的突破，如何将其与结构化知识库和推理能力结合，并进一步延伸到物理世界的具身智能，成为迈向通用具身智能的关键科学问题。该综述旨在系统梳理这一融合趋势，为开发者提供路线图。

Method: 采用综述研究方法：首先分析LLM的架构、预训练方法和推理机制；其次探讨LLM与外部知识源及结构化推理框架的交互；再次考察具身智能范式（代理在物理环境中学习和行动）；最后提出一个概念性框架整合上述维度，并归纳五大挑战。

Result: 提出了一个概念性框架展示LLM、知识库、推理能力和具身化的协同关系（作为感知、推理和行动的指导模型而非工程实现架构），并识别了迈向通用具身智能的五大关键挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述为开发能够适应复杂动态环境的自适应多模态智能代理提供了全面路线图，指出LLM与知识库、推理和具身化的深度融合是通往通用具身智能的关键路径。

Abstract: The convergence of large language models (LLMs), structured knowledge bases (KBs), and reasoning ability (RA) presents a promising trajectory toward general embodied intelligence (GEI). This paper reviews the evolution of LLM-centered intelligent systems, emphasising their integration with knowledge representation, logical reasoning, and physical embodiment. We analyse LLM architectures, pre-training methods, and inference mechanisms, along with their interaction with external knowledge sources and structured reasoning frameworks. Furthermore, we examine embodied intelligence (EI) paradigms wherein agents learn and act in physical environments. To synthesise these dimensions, we present a conceptual framework that illustrates the synergy among LLMs, KBs, RA, and embodiment, serving as a guiding model for perception, reasoning, and action rather than an implemented engineering architecture. To advance toward GEI, we identify five key challenges: efficient LLM deployment, closed-loop knowledge integration, hybrid symbolic-neural reasoning, perception-action grounding, and continual learning. This survey provides a comprehensive roadmap for developing adaptive, multimodal agents capable of operating in complex, dynamic settings.

</details>


### [95] [ADAPT: Physics-Aware Diffusion-based World Models for Adaptive Predictive Transferable HVAC Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19804)
*Xu Yang,Kailai Sun,Dianyu Zhong,Qianchuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT，一种物理感知的条件扩散室内环境世界模型，用于HVAC（暖通空调）控制。该模型通过捕捉建筑热惯性、利用扩散模型的生成鲁棒性以及多区域热平衡正则化，解决了室内热力学延迟响应、部分可观测性和分布外泛化问题，在能量消耗和居住舒适度方面均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能耗占全球约三分之一，优化室内气候系统对实现联合国可持续发展目标11和13至关重要。然而，现有方法受限于室内热动力学的延迟响应和部分可观测性，面临热惯性隐式建模困难、占用动态预测不准和累积预测误差等问题。尤其是在分布外环境下，密集传感器部署成本高且涉及隐私问题，导致数据仅在单一运行工况下收集，而控制器需要在未见过的季节和气候区域中可靠泛化。

Method: ADAPT包含三个核心组件：（1）短时域"持行动热基线"预测模块，用于捕捉建筑潜在热惯性；（2）条件扩散模型作为主干，利用生成模型的鲁棒性来建模室内环境动态；（3）可学习的多区域热平衡正则化器，在不需要已知建筑几何结构或手动标定热参数的情况下，约束生成的轨迹满足可迁移的建筑热力学规律。此外，为下游强化学习设计了信用分配机制。

Result: 在SemibuildingSim和Sinergym上的大量实验表明：在IID（同分布）控制场景下，ADAPT相比最先进基线减少了7.3%的HVAC能耗和30.2%的居住者不适度；在覆盖未见季节和气候区域的OOD（分布外）控制场景下，ADAPT保持了稳健性能，仅相对其IID性能有微弱退化，在迁移鲁棒性上大幅超越现有方法。

Conclusion: ADAPT通过物理感知的条件扩散世界模型有效解决了HVAC控制中热惯性建模和分布外泛化两大核心挑战，在提升能源效率和居住舒适度的同时展示了出色的迁移鲁棒性，为实际建筑中的低密度传感、跨区域部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Buildings account for roughly one-third of global energy consumption and CO$_2$ emissions. Optimizing indoor climate systems plays a critical role for urban climate mitigation aligned with UN Sustainable Development Goals 11 and 13. However, indoor delayed thermodynamic responses and partial observability severely hinder existing methods, which are primarily limited by implicit thermal inertia, occupancy dynamic prediction, and cumulative prediction errors, especially for out-of-distribution environments. In practice, these challenges are further exacerbated by the high cost and privacy burden of dense indoor sensing, forcing operators to collect only limited data in a single operating regime while expecting controllers to generalize reliably across unseen seasons and climate regions. To address this problem, we propose ADAPT, a physics-aware conditional diffusion indoor environmental world model for HVAC control. The model predicts a short-horizon held-action thermal baseline to capture the latent thermal inertia of the buildings. The diffusion backbone utilizes the robustness of generative models, while a learnable multi-zone heat-balance regularizer constrains generated trajectories to satisfy transferable building thermodynamics without requiring known building geometry or manually calibrated thermal parameters. A credit assignment is then design for the downstream reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments on SemibuildingSim and Sinergym demonstrate that ADAPT reduces HVAC energy consumption by 7.3\% and occupant discomfort by 30.2\% compared with state-of-the-art baselines under IID control. Under OOD control scenarios spanning unseen seasons and climate regions, ADAPT maintains robust performance with only marginal degradation relative to its IID performance, substantially outperforming existing methods in transfer robustness.

</details>


### [96] [When Saying No Makes Better Videos: Designing Dual Gatekeeping for Pedagogically Grounded AI Content Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19812)
*Yearim Kim,Njun Baek,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个视频创作流水线，通过两层结构化"拒绝"（教育者基于多媒体学习理论迭代重构AI脚本 + 自动化指标检测教学连贯性和叙事-视觉同步性）来防止生成美学上精致但教学上有缺陷的AI内容。实验表明两层独立改进了相同的教学维度，证明审慎的对抗与生成式AI并非对立而是伙伴关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防止AI生成内容虽然外观精美但教学上存在缺陷，需要一种机制在采用前进行严格的质量把关。

Method: 提出一个包含两层结构化拒绝的视频创作流水线：第一层让教育者基于多媒体学习理论迭代改造AI生成的脚本；第二层采用自动化指标标记教学连贯性和叙事-视觉同步性的违规行为。

Result: 对23位教育者在3个主题上的研究以及跨7个主题（源自科学和哲学课程）的自动化指标评估表明，两层分别独立地改进了相同的教学维度。

Conclusion: 两层虽不完整但协同作用，使"原则性抵抗"（延迟接受AI输出直到满足严格标准）成为提升质量的催化剂；审慎的抵抗与生成式AI是可以互补的。

Abstract: To prevent the adoption of aesthetically polished but pedagogically flawed AI content, we study a video authoring pipeline featuring two layers of structured refusal. The first layer empowers educators to iteratively reshape AI scripts based on multimedia learning theory, while the second employs automated metrics to flag violations in instructional coherence and narrative-visual synchronization. While neither layer is exhaustive, their synergy ensures that principled resistance--the act of deferring AI output until it meets rigorous standards--becomes a catalyst for higher quality. Evaluation combining a study with 23 educators across 3 topics and automated metrics across 7 topics drawn from established science and philosophy curricula shows that both layers independently improve the same instructional dimensions, suggesting that thoughtful resistance and generative AI are not opposites but partners.

</details>


### [97] [Causal Reasoning with Bipartite Graphical Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19831)
*Joris M. Mooij*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出双分图因果模型（BGCMs），通过双分图编码方程组的结构，解决了传统因果贝叶斯网络（CBNs）和结构因果模型（SCMs）无法表示的平衡系统（含循环因果依赖）中完美干预do(X=x)的歧义问题，并引入了新的图分离准则B-separation，严格推广了CBNs和SCMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CBNs和SCMs框架无法充分表示所有真实世界的因果系统，特别是处于平衡状态的系统，其中反馈机制会产生循环因果依赖。在这些系统中，不同的干预即使施加相同的变量值也可能产生不同的效果，使得标准"完美干预"do(X=x)的语义变得含糊不清。

Method: 作者提出双分图因果模型（BGCMs），其中方程系统的结构通过包含变量节点和方程节点的双分图来编码。硬干预do(f_j: X_v = ξ_v)明确指定了被替换的方程、被干预的目标变量以及干预值，从而消除了标准干预概念的歧义。作者定义了新的图分离准则（B-separation），利用方程中的函数确定性来表述马尔可夫性质，并将其扩展到非随机输入设置，进而推导出用于域不变性推理的do-calculus。

Result: 通过对物理系统的详细案例研究，作者证明该表示自然地对应了不同的真实世界干预。BGCMs严格推广了CBNs和SCMs，同时保留了执行图形化因果推理的能力。

Conclusion: BGCMs为表示涉及平衡或反馈机制的因果系统提供了更丰富的框架，解决了传统模型在干预语义方面的局限性，并为图形化因果推理提供了一般化的理论工具。

Abstract: Causal Bayesian networks (CBNs) and structural causal models (SCMs) are the dominant frameworks for graphical causal reasoning, but they cannot adequately represent all real-world causal systems. In particular, systems at equilibrium---where feedback mechanisms create cyclic causal dependencies---can exhibit causal semantics that are fundamentally incompatible with these frameworks: different interventions that enforce the same variable value may have different effects, rendering the standard ``perfect intervention'' do($X = x$) ambiguous. We propose bipartite graphical causal models (BGCMs), in which the structure of a system of equations is encoded by a bipartite graph with variable and equation nodes. In this framework, a hard intervention do($f_j : X_v = ξ_v$) specifies which equation is replaced, which variable is targeted, and at what value---resolving the ambiguity of the standard notion. We demonstrate, through a detailed case study of a physical system, that this representation naturally corresponds to distinct real-world interventions. We formulate a Markov property in terms of a new graphical separation criterion (B-separation) that exploits the functional determinism inherent in the equations, and we extend it to settings with non-random inputs. We show how this gives rise to a do-calculus for reasoning about domain invariances. BGCMs strictly generalize CBNs and SCMs while retaining the ability to perform graphical causal reasoning.

</details>


### [98] [Specification-delta-driven data governance: an empirical study of the «spec-delta» as the unit of change in lakehouse data platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19838)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了"规范增量"(spec-delta)概念，提出数据平台变更分类法，并设计对照实验，在真实湖仓环境中对比spec-delta驱动工作流与传统代码PR工作流的效率差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然"规范驱动开发"(SDD)在软件领域已被广泛认可（如GitHub Spec Kit、Constitutional SDD），且数据契约文献已将其扩展到运行时模式与质量强制，但OpenSpec提出的"规范增量"——即每次变更都应产生可评审的需求增量——作为数据平台变更单元的核心思想，在实证层面仍然空白。大量数据平台变更是契约性质的（新数据集、SLA、指标语义、访问策略）而非纯代码变更，因此需要系统验证该理念的有效性。

Method: 形式化定义spec-delta概念；提出按增量规范适用性对数据平台变更进行分类的分类法；设计对照实验，比较spec-delta驱动工作流与无delta的传统代码PR工作流。响应变量包括：发现到部署的时间、到达Silver和Gold湖仓层的缺陷密度、跨工具指标分歧、以及用NASA TLX测量的评审者认知负荷。论文预留了演示与实验室章节用于在真实湖仓环境中的实例化。

Result: 论文明确提出其贡献不是工具，而是可复现的实验证据和应用指南；实验协议已设计并预留真实湖仓环境的实例化章节，最终结果有待实验执行。

Conclusion: 本文旨在通过受控实验提供经验证据，展示spec-delta驱动工作流在数据平台场景中的价值，并提供应用指南以避免"前期过度规范化"(up-front over-specification)的反模式。

Abstract: Spec Driven Development SDD has consolidated the idea that the specification rather than the code should be the primary artefact governing AI assisted work. Tools such as GitHub Spec Kit, and proposals such as Constitutional SDD, have formalised this principle in the software domain, while the executable data-contracts literature has extended it to schema and quality enforcement at run time. Nevertheless, the treatment of the specification delta OpenSpec's core idea that every change should produce a reviewable increment of requirements as the unit of change in data platforms remains empirically unexplored, even though many data-platform changes are contractual (new datasets, service-level agreements, metric semantics, access policies) rather than purely code changes. This work formalises the spec-delta concept, proposes a taxonomy of data platform changes according to their suitability for incremental specification, and defines a controlled experiment comparing a spec-delta-driven workflow against a conventional code pull-request workflow without a delta. The response variables are discovery to deployment time, the density of defects reaching the Silver and Gold lakehouse layers, cross-tool metric divergence, and reviewer cognitive load measured with NASA TLX.
